id=\"offerings_update_form_3999_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDoraHacks Global Hackathon Series 2020-2021 (GHS) is hosting the 3rd hackathon Mar 6-7 2021. The first hackathon was Defi Hackathon in Nov 2020, the second was Substrate - Polkadot Hackathon in December. You can now view all projects on HackerLink BUIDL page (\u003ca href=\"https://hackerlink.io/en/buidl\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://hackerlink.io/en/buidl\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe GHS 2021 will be hosted in China (March), Singapore (April), US (April -- July), India (TBD), Germany (TBD), Vietnam (Online), and Russia (Online). A full schedule will be shared soon on our medium (\u003ca href=\"https://hidorahacks.medium.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://hidorahacks.medium.com\u003c/a\u003e). \u003cstrong\u003ePlease join us if you are around and meet us at the hackathons!\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe March hackathon will be\u003cstrong\u003e ETH Hackathon -- DAO, NFT, Layer-2, in Beijing\u003c/strong\u003e. We are adding a DAOFest, an NFTFest to this hackathon. In addition, we are going to use quadratic voting to pick up winning team this time. Details can be found here: \u003ca href=\"https://hidorahacks.medium.com/eth-hackathon-nft-dao-layer-2-beijing-3d5a3c36dc0\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://hidorahacks.medium.com/eth-hackathon-nft-dao-layer-2-beijing-3d5a3c36dc0\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe NFTFest is partnered with Whale, Flow, iNFT, Mintbase, BCA, and CryptoArt.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/035/162/35162-1613762106-7ea7d1b5dc97f7d9ef99ace79eff8de2a0c5801d.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\" style=\"width: 429px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBinance Smart Chain is going to sponsor 10 of 15 hackathons from the GHS, in China, US, and India. id=\"offerings_update_form_1453_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eA DAO is a decentralized autonomous organization. In contrast to a normal company / organization, a DAO is a vehicle to coordinate and incentivize crowd behavior to achieve a measurable goal. (For detailed description of DAO, see \u003ca href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Decentralized_autonomous_organization\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Decentralized_autonomous_organization\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHackathon and the hacker movement are intrinsically aligned with DAO, both spiritually and practically. Spiritually, the hacker movement does not have any central coordination. Practically, we have found many interesting ideas of DAOs that could help us organize developer communities better.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith the help from DAOStack team (a DAO infrastructure project that helps decentralized organizations create and manage DAOs on Ethereum), we deployed a DAO that supports student hackathon organizers and named it HackerDAO A. We will become one of the first supporters of this DAO. Students who have hackathon proposals could apply for grants via this DAO and get funded to organize their own hackathons, after fulfilling a certain set of criteria and getting passed by a simple majority vote in the DAO. The goal of HackerDAO A is to eventually support a large number of student hackathons every year with community efforts.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHackerDAO will become a collection of DAOs that implement different ideas to support hacker movement. HackerDAO A is the first one.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDuring our campaign, we received many warm messages from our investors. Some of you are open source contributors, some of you have sons and daughters who are into computer science and open source developments. If you or your friends / families have hackathon ideas and want to get involved by organizing a hackathon, please do not hesitate to contact us through messages on Republic.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eJiannan\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 id=\"offerings_update_form_1134_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eFrom all of us at DoraHacks, thank you!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIt has been a great two weeks; we have successfully raised over $18,000 from 63 savvy investors. We’re very excited to see new investors and hackers joining our journey.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDoraHacks Co-hosted a Blockchain Hackathon with Wanxiang Group in Shanghai, China\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLast weekend, DoraHacks helped Wanxiang Group host and organize its blockchain hackathon during Shanghai International Blockchain Week. It was hugely successful! There were more than 120 blockchain developers, formed into 28 teams, attending the event. The theme of the hackathon was “The Application of Blockchain in Automotive Industry”. Hackers spent a total of 26 hours, from the ground up, creating innovative projects and were determined to win the six awards provided by Wanxiang Group.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe StormBird team won the top prize in the competition with its excellent creativity and high degree of completion. The award included RMB 50,000 and four tickets to the rest of the summit. Their project, “Karma Car Owner Token”, uses blockchain technology to tokenize automobile assets, allowing low friction in the market for automobile trading, sharing, getting a mortgage and so on.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/011/701/11701-1568812518-983d760231dd79248a379c4e214bdd4feaf700b1.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn the next few weeks, DoraHacks is going to \u003cstrong\u003eannounce a series of meetups\u003c/strong\u003e ahead of our Hackathon in December. Please stay tuned and follow DoraHacks on \u003ca href=\"http://linkedin.com/company/dorahacks\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eLinkedIn\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"https://twitter.com/DoraHacks\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eTwitter\u003c/a\u003e, and \u003ca href=\"https://www.facebook.com/DoraHacksGlobalHack/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eFacebook\u003c/a\u003e for more information.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe absolutely love what we do, and we’re very excited to start a revolution that will connect talented hackers/developers to top enterprises and fundamentally change the traditional way of tech recruiting!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you again for the opportunity to build something According to our founder, Jiannan Zhang, tokenizing DoraHacks’ preferred shares can offer investors with maximum flexibility.
